⏺ Designing the Custom Aliases Feature

  Let's review the requirements:
  1. API / Backend Side (src/index.ts):
     - ShortenPayload can accept optional alias?: string (or customAlias?: 
       string).
     - Alias validation rules:
       - If provided and not empty:
         - Trim it.
         - Length check: e.g. 3 to 50 characters. If too short or too long:
           return 400 with helpful error message (e.g. "Alias must be between 3
           and 50 characters.").
         - Character check: regex ^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$. If invalid: return 400
           ("Alias can only contain letters, numbers, hyphens, and
           underscores.").
         - Reserved routes check: Cannot be "api", "links", "favicon.ico",
           "robots.txt", "shorten", etc.
         - Duplicate check:
           Query D1: SELECT code FROM urls WHERE code = ? LIMIT 1
           (case-insensitive or exact).
           And check UrlShortenerService.memoryStore.has(cleanAlias).
           If exists: return status 409 (Conflict) with { success: false, error:
           "The alias '${cleanAlias}' is already in use. Please choose another 
           one." } or { success: false, error: "Custom alias '${cleanAlias}' 
           already exists." }.
       - If alias is NOT provided: generate 6-character random code as before.
       - When saving: save using the alias as code!
       - In createShortUrl(rawUrl, baseUrl, env, expiresInMs, customAlias?):
         Return { success: false, error: "...", status?: 400 | 409 } if
         validation fails or duplicate.
       - In handlePostShorten:
         Pass customAlias (or alias) to createShortUrl. If duplicate/conflict,
         return HTTP 409 (or whatever result.status || 400).
  2. Frontend UI Side:
     - In src/client/types.ts:
       - Update ShortenUrlRequest:
  export interface ShortenUrlRequest {
      url: string;
      expiresIn?: number;
      alias?: string;
  }
     - In src/client/services/api.ts:
       - Update shortenUrl:
  async shortenUrl(
      originalUrl: string,
      expiresIn: number = 86400000,
      alias?: string,
  ): Promise<{ code: string; shortUrl: string; data?: ShortenUrlResponseData }>
  {
      const body: { url: string; expiresIn: number; alias?: string } = {
          url: originalUrl.trim(),
          expiresIn,
      };
      if (alias && alias.trim()) {
          body.alias = alias.trim();
      }
      const response = await fetch("/api/shorten", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
              "Content-Type": "application/json",
              Accept: "application/json",
          },
          body: JSON.stringify(body),
      });
      ...
     - In src/client/components/UrlForm.tsx:
       - Add alias state: const [alias, setAlias] = useState<string>("");
       - Add aliasError state: const [aliasError, setAliasError] = 
         useState<string | null>(null);
       - Validate alias on change/blur:
         If alias is entered, check ^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$ and length 3-50 chars.
       - Add Custom Alias input field in the form:
         - Clear, modern UI with prefix (e.g. your-domain/ [custom-alias]),
           placeholder "e.g. my-promo-link".
         - Badge saying "(Optional)".
         - Error display if format is invalid.
       - Pass alias.trim() to urlApiClient.shortenUrl(url, expiresInMs, 
         alias.trim()).
       - Handle duplicate alias errors from API gracefully, showing the error
         message in apiError.
  3. Inactive/Expired Screen Redirect Bug Fix:
     - In src/index.ts:
       Notice renderErrorPage(status, error, description, frontendUrl):
       Currently:
       <a href="/" class="btn">Go to URL Shortener</a>
       When the user runs the app, the frontend Vite app is on
       http://localhost:5173 (or whatever origin / FRONTEND_URL / request
       referer).
       If someone accesses a short URL via http://localhost:8787/expir1 and sees
       the 404 or 410 HTML screen, clicking "Go to URL Shortener" was
       navigating to / on the worker (http://localhost:8787/), which only
       returned "Hello World!"!
       We should compute frontendUrl:
  public static getFrontendUrl(request?: Request, env?: Env): string {
      if ((env as any)?.FRONTEND_URL) {
          return (env as any).FRONTEND_URL;
      }
      if (request) {
          const origin = request.headers.get("Origin");
          if (origin && origin !== "null") return origin;
          const referer = request.headers.get("Referer");
          if (referer) {
              try {
                  const parsed = new URL(referer);
                  if (parsed.origin) return parsed.origin;
              } catch {}
          }
          const host = request.headers.get("Host") || new URL(request.url).host;
          if (host.includes("localhost:8787") ||
  host.includes("127.0.0.1:8787")) {
              return "http://localhost:5173";
          }
      }
      return "http://localhost:5173";
  }
       And in renderErrorPage:
       <a href="${frontendUrl}" class="btn">Go to URL Shortener</a>
       AND in the worker fetch handler:
       When rendering error page:
       Pass frontendUrl = UrlShortenerService.getFrontendUrl(request, env);
       UrlShortenerService.renderErrorPage(redirectResult.status, 
       redirectResult.error, redirectResult.description, frontendUrl).
       AND what if a user navigates to GET / with browser Accept: text/html?
       Wait, in test/index.spec.ts:
       it("responds with Hello World! on default GET request", async () => { ...
       expect(await response.text()).toMatchInlineSnapshot('"Hello World!"'); 
       });
       So GET / still returns "Hello World!" (or if it's test request without
       Accept: text/html). But the link in the error page specifically points to
       frontendUrl!
       Let's check the user request wording:
       "Beside this, there is a bug with the inactive/expired screenn: Go to URL
       Shortener is redirecting to the api root instead frontend root screen.
       Fix it."
       The button was literally <a href="/" class="btn">Go to URL Shortener</a>!
       Changing it to <a href="${frontendUrl}" class="btn">Go to URL 
       Shortener</a> directly fixes this bug!
  4. Unit Tests (test/index.spec.ts):
     - Add tests for:
       i. Creating a short URL with a custom alias succeeds and shortUrl
          contains the custom alias (POST /api/shorten with { url, alias: 
          "custom-promo" }).
       ii. Attempting to create a short URL with an already existing alias
           returns error (status 409 or 400) for duplicated entries.
       iii. Invalid alias formats (e.g. containing spaces, symbols like !@#$%,
            or less than 3 characters) are rejected with status 400.
       iv. Reserved alias names (e.g. "api", "links") are rejected with
           status 400.
       v. Short URLs created with custom aliases redirect properly via GET 
          /:alias and track analytics correctly.
       vi. Verify that the 410 inactive/expired HTML screen contains the link
           pointing to the frontend URL (e.g. http://localhost:5173) rather than
           just "/".

⏺ Checking checking code existence in D1 & memoryStore

  Let's write a helper method in UrlShortenerService:
  public static async codeExists(code: string, env?: Env): Promise<boolean> {
      if (UrlShortenerService.memoryStore.has(code)) {
          return true;
      }
      if (env?.url_shortener_db) {
          try {
              await UrlShortenerService.initDatabase(env.url_shortener_db);
              const row = await env.url_shortener_db
                  .prepare("SELECT code FROM urls WHERE code = ? LIMIT 1")
                  .bind(code)
                  .first<{ code: string }>();
              if (row && row.code) {
                  return true;
              }
          } catch {
              // DB query failed or table not found
          }
      }
      return false;
  }

  And a validation method for custom aliases:
  public static validateAlias(alias: unknown): { isValid: boolean; error?:
  string; cleanAlias?: string } {
      if (alias === undefined || alias === null || alias === "") {
          return { isValid: true };
      }
      if (typeof alias !== "string") {
          return { isValid: false, error: "Alias must be a string." };
      }
      const clean = alias.trim();
      if (clean.length === 0) {
          return { isValid: true };
      }
      if (clean.length < 3) {
          return { isValid: false, error: "Custom alias must be at least 3 
  characters long." };
      }
      if (clean.length > 50) {
          return { isValid: false, error: "Custom alias cannot exceed 50 
  characters." };
      }
      if (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$/.test(clean)) {
          return { isValid: false, error: "Custom alias can only contain 
  letters, numbers, hyphens, and underscores." };
      }
      const reserved = ["api", "links", "shorten", "urls", "validate",
  "analytics", "favicon.ico", "robots.txt"];
      if (reserved.includes(clean.toLowerCase())) {
          return { isValid: false, error: `The alias '${clean}' is reserved and
  cannot be used.` };
      }
      return { isValid: true, cleanAlias: clean };
  }

  Then in createShortUrl:
  public static async createShortUrl(
      rawUrl: string,
      baseUrl: string,
      env?: Env,
      expiresInMs: number = UrlShortenerService.DEFAULT_EXPIRES_IN_MS,
      customAlias?: string,
  ): Promise<ServiceResponse<ShortenedUrlRecord>> {
      const validation = UrlShortenerService.validateUrl(rawUrl);

      if (!validation.isValid || !validation.normalizedUrl) {
          return {
              success: false,
              error: validation.error || "Invalid URL",
              statusCode: 400,
          };
      }

      let code: string;

      if (customAlias && customAlias.trim().length > 0) {
          const aliasValidation =
  UrlShortenerService.validateAlias(customAlias);
          if (!aliasValidation.isValid || !aliasValidation.cleanAlias) {
              return {
                  success: false,
                  error: aliasValidation.error || "Invalid alias format.",
                  statusCode: 400,
              };
          }

          const candidate = aliasValidation.cleanAlias;
          const exists = await UrlShortenerService.codeExists(candidate, env);
          if (exists) {
              return {
                  success: false,
                  error: `The alias '${candidate}' is already in use. Please
  choose another one.`,
                  statusCode: 409,
              };
          }
          code = candidate;
      } else {
          code = UrlShortenerService.generateShortCode();
      }
      ...

  Notice ServiceResponse<T>:
  export interface ServiceResponse<T> {
      success: boolean;
      data?: T;
      error?: string;
      statusCode?: number;
  }

  export interface ShortenPayload {

      expiresIn?: number;
      alias?: string;
      customAlias?: string;
  }

  const requestedAlias = payload.alias || payload.customAlias;
  const result = await UrlShortenerService.createShortUrl(
      payload.url,
      baseUrl,
      env,
      expiresIn,
      requestedAlias,
  );

  if (!result.success || !result.data) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify(result), {
          status: result.statusCode || 400,
          headers: {
              "Content-Type": "application/json",
              ...corsHeaders,
          },
      });
  }
  If the alias is already in use, it will return HTTP 409 with { success: false,
  error: "The alias '...' is already in use. Please choose another one." }.

  Let's check what UrlShortenerService.getFrontendUrl(request, env) should be:
  public static getFrontendUrl(request?: Request, env?: Env): string {
      if ((env as any)?.FRONTEND_URL) {
          return (env as any).FRONTEND_URL;
      }
      if (request) {
          const origin = request.headers.get("Origin");
          if (origin && origin !== "null") return origin;
          const referer = request.headers.get("Referer");
          if (referer) {
              try {
                  const parsed = new URL(referer);
                  if (parsed.origin) return parsed.origin;
              } catch {}
          }
          const host = request.headers.get("Host") || new URL(request.url).host;
          // In local development or testing with port 8787, redirect to 
  frontend on port 5173
          if (host.includes("localhost:8787") ||
  host.includes("127.0.0.1:8787")) {
              return "http://localhost:5173";
          }
      }
      return "http://localhost:5173";
  }
  Let's verify: In local development, the user visits http://localhost:8787/code
  in the browser. The response is the HTML error page. When they click the
  button "Go to URL Shortener", previously <a href="/"> sent them to
  http://localhost:8787/ (which returned "Hello World!").
  Now, with <a href="http://localhost:5173" class="btn">Go to URL Shortener</a>,
  clicking the button takes them directly to the frontend root screen at
  http://localhost:5173!

⏺ Writing validateAlias, codeExists, and updating createShortUrl

  Let's review what validation should be done for alias:
  - If alias is undefined, null, or empty string, it's valid (optional).
  - If provided, trim it.
  - Length: min 3, max 50 characters.
  - Characters: only alphanumeric, hyphens, and underscores: /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$/.
  - Reserved: "api", "links", "shorten", "urls", "validate", "analytics",
    "favicon.ico", "robots.txt".
  - Duplicate check:
    If codeExists(cleanAlias, env):
    Return error: The alias '${cleanAlias}' is already in use. Please choose 
    another one. with statusCode: 409.

⏺ Updating createShortUrl to support custom aliases

  Let's check lines 267 to 330 in src/index.ts.
  In createShortUrl:
        public static async createShortUrl(
                rawUrl: string,
                baseUrl: string,
                env?: Env,
                expiresInMs: number = UrlShortenerService.DEFAULT_EXPIRES_IN_MS,
                customAlias?: string,
        ): Promise<ServiceResponse<ShortenedUrlRecord>> {
                const validation = UrlShortenerService.validateUrl(rawUrl);

                if (!validation.isValid || !validation.normalizedUrl) {
                        return {
                                success: false,
                                error: validation.error || "Invalid URL",
                                statusCode: 400,
                        };
                }

                let code: string;

                if (customAlias && typeof customAlias === "string" &&
  customAlias.trim().length > 0) {
                        const aliasValidation = UrlShortenerService.validateAlia
                        if (!aliasValidation.isValid || !aliasValidation.cleanAl
                                return {
                                        success: false,
                                        error: aliasValidation.error || "Invalid
                                        statusCode: 400,
                                };
                        }

                        const candidate = aliasValidation.cleanAlias;
                        const exists = await UrlShortenerService.codeExists(cand
                        if (exists) {
                                return {
                                        success: false,
                                        error: `The alias '${candidate}' is alre
  one.`,
                                        statusCode: 409,
                                };
                        }
                        code = candidate;
                } else {
                        code = UrlShortenerService.generateShortCode();
                }

                // Ensure expiresInMs is a positive integer
                const safeExpiresIn = typeof expiresInMs === "number" && expires
                        ? expiresInMs
                        : UrlShortenerService.DEFAULT_EXPIRES_IN_MS;

                const cleanBase = baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, "");
                const shortUrl = `${cleanBase}/${code}`;
                const now = new Date();
                const expiresAtDate = new Date(now.getTime() + safeExpiresIn);

                const record: ShortenedUrlRecord = {
                        code,
                        originalUrl: validation.normalizedUrl,
                        shortUrl,
                        clicks: 0,
                        createdAt: now.toISOString(),
                        expiresAt: expiresAtDate.toISOString(),
                        active: true,
                };

⏺ Implementing getFrontendUrl and updating renderErrorPage

  Let's check getFrontendUrl:
        /**
         * Determines the frontend root URL based on request headers, environmen
  default local port
         */
        public static getFrontendUrl(request?: Request, env?: Env): string {
                if ((env as Record<string, unknown>)?.FRONTEND_URL && typeof (en
  Record<string, unknown>).FRONTEND_URL === "string") {
                        return (env as Record<string, string>).FRONTEND_URL;
                }

                if (request) {
                        const origin = request.headers.get("Origin");
                        if (origin && origin !== "null") return origin;

                        const referer = request.headers.get("Referer");
                        if (referer) {
                                try {
                                        const parsed = new URL(referer);
                                        if (parsed.origin) return parsed.origin;
                                } catch {
                                        // Ignore invalid referer
                                }
                        }

                        const host = request.headers.get("Host") || "";
                        if (host.includes("localhost:8787") || host.includes("12
                                return "http://localhost:5173";
                        }
                }

                return "http://localhost:5173";
        }

  const validateAlias = (value: string): string | null => {
      const trimmed = value.trim();
      if (!trimmed) return null; // Optional
      if (trimmed.length < 3) {
          return "Custom alias must be at least 3 characters.";
      }
      if (trimmed.length > 50) {
          return "Custom alias cannot exceed 50 characters.";
      }
      if (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$/.test(trimmed)) {
          return "Alias can only contain letters, numbers, hyphens, and 
  underscores.";
      }
      return null;
  };

⏺ Adding alias change/blur handlers and updating handleSubmit

  In src/client/components/UrlForm.tsx:
        const handleAliasChange = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
                const nextValue = e.target.value;
                setAlias(nextValue);
                setApiError(null);

                if (isAliasTouched) {
                        setAliasError(validateAlias(nextValue));
                }
        };

        const handleAliasBlur = () => {
                setIsAliasTouched(true);
                setAliasError(validateAlias(alias));
        };

        const handleSubmit = async (e: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                setIsTouched(true);
                setIsAliasTouched(true);

                const urlErr = validateUrl(url);
                const alErr = validateAlias(alias);

                if (urlErr || alErr) {
                        setValidationError(urlErr);
                        setAliasError(alErr);
                        return;
                }

                setValidationError(null);
                setAliasError(null);
                setApiError(null);
                setIsSubmitting(true);
                setCopied(false);

                const expiresInMs = getEffectiveExpiresIn();

                try {
                        const result = await urlApiClient.shortenUrl(url, expire
  || undefined);
                        setLastSuccess(result);
                        setUrl("");
                        setAlias("");
                        setIsTouched(false);
                        setIsAliasTouched(false);
                        if (onUrlCreated) {
                                onUrlCreated(result);
                        }
                } catch (err) {
                        const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Un
  shortening URL.";
                        setApiError(message);
                } finally {
                        setIsSubmitting(false);
                }
        };

⏺ Writing comprehensive tests for Custom Aliases and Expired Redirect Fix

  Let's design unit tests in test/index.spec.ts:
  1. In Edge Cases & Validation Resilience:
     Check the HTML button in 410 screen links to frontend root:
  expect(html).toContain('href="http://localhost:5173"');
  expect(html).not.toContain('href="/"');
  2. A new describe("Custom Aliases Feature", () => { ... }):
     - POST /api/shorten with alias: saves custom alias when code does not exist
       - Status 201
       - json.code === "my-promo"
       - json.shortUrl === "http://localhost:8787/my-promo"
       - Redirect GET /my-promo returns 302 to target url
     - POST /api/shorten with alias: returns 409 conflict when alias already 
       exists
       - First request with alias "vip-deal" succeeds (201)
       - Second request with alias "vip-deal" returns 409
       - Error indicates alias is already in use
     - POST /api/shorten with alias: rejects invalid alias formats (spaces, 
       special chars, too short < 3, too long > 50)
       - Rejects "ab" (too short, status 400)
       - Rejects "invalid alias with spaces" (status 400)
       - Rejects "hello@world!" (status 400)
       - Rejects "a".repeat(55) (status 400)
     - POST /api/shorten with alias: rejects reserved alias names
       - "api" (status 400)
       - "links" (status 400)
     - POST /api/shorten: alias is optional, generates random code when omitted 
       or empty
       - When alias is omitted, generates random code (length 6)
       - When alias: "" (empty string), generates random code (length 6)
     - Inactive/expired HTML error screen: "Go to URL Shortener" button 
       redirects to frontend root instead of API root
       - Verifies directRes.text() has <a href="http://localhost:5173" 
         class="btn">Go to URL Shortener</a>
       - Verifies custom FRONTEND_URL environment override if set.

  Resumen de cambios:

  1. Aliases Personalizados en API (src/index.ts):
     - ShortenPayload acepta alias?: string (o customAlias).
     - Validación de alias (UrlShortenerService.validateAlias):
       - Opcional: si no se envía o está vacío, genera código aleatorio de 6
         caracteres.
       - Longitud entre 3 y 50 caracteres.
       - Solo caracteres alfanuméricos, guiones y guiones bajos
         (^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$).
       - Protección contra rutas reservadas (api, links, shorten, validate,
         analytics, etc.).
     - Verificación de duplicados (UrlShortenerService.codeExists): consulta
       Cloudflare D1 y memoryStore.
     - Si el alias ya existe, responde con código 409 Conflict e información
       descriptiva.
     - Si no existe, almacena el alias como code y genera la URL corta
       correspondiente (/:alias).
  2. Formulario Frontend (src/client/components/UrlForm.tsx & 
     src/client/services/api.ts):
     - Nuevo campo Custom Alias (Optional) con prefijo / y limpieza rápida.
     - Validación reactiva en cliente (longitud, caracteres permitidos).
     - Manejo de respuestas 409 y 400 mostrando mensaje de error en la interfaz.
  3. Corrección de Redirección en Pantalla Inactiva / Expirada (src/index.ts):
     - El botón "Go to URL Shortener" apuntaba a / en la raíz del worker
       (http://localhost:8787/), que mostraba "Hello World!".
     - Implementado UrlShortenerService.getFrontendUrl para resolver la URL del
       frontend (http://localhost:5173 en desarrollo, cabeceras Origin/Referer,
       o variable FRONTEND_URL).
     - El botón en renderErrorPage ahora redirige directamente a la pantalla
       principal del frontend.
  4. Pruebas Unitarias (test/index.spec.ts):
     - 22 pruebas ejecutadas y aprobadas (22 passed en Vitest):
       - Creación y redirección 302 con alias personalizado.
       - Detección de duplicados con status 409.
       - Rechazo de formatos inválidos (espacios, caracteres especiales,
         longitud < 3 o > 50).
       - Bloqueo de nombres de ruta reservados.
       - Comportamiento opcional con generación aleatoria.
       - Verificación del enlace del botón "Go to URL Shortener" hacia la URL
         del frontend.

✻ Worked for 12m 25s · done 11:54 AM

※ recap: Construimos el acortador de URLs en Cloudflare Workers y React, y terminamos los alias personalizados con el botón corregido. Todo compila con 22 pruebas aprobadas; define el siguiente paso o nueva funcionalidad por implementar. (disable recaps in /config)
